- **nix-packages**: Make the package half of a config-change in the hypatia-tile/nix-packages checkout beside this repository — add or remove a Nix package, a Homebrew cask or brew, or a font — on a feature branch, committed through nix-packages' hooks and checked with its bin/preflight.sh, then report the branch and commits. Nothing is pushed. From a dotfiles-mac session, this is the only way nix-packages is changed (ADR 0038). ([hypatia-tile/dotfiles-mac](https://github.com/hypatia-tile/dotfiles-mac/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nix-packages.md))

Invoke with @nix-pkg or delegate when the user says "add X to my nix config" / "package &lt;tool&gt; for me" / "update the overlay for &lt;pkg&gt;". ([DWSR/nix-config](https://github.com/DWSR/nix-config/blob/HEAD/home-manager/common/agents/nix-pkg.md))

- **nix-reviewer**: Reviews .nix changes for correctness, purity, security and repo conventions - module merging, option types, fetchers and hashes, IFD and impurity, secrets or user paths in the store, systemd hardening, Home Manager pitfalls, platform guards - and runs statix, deadnix and nixfmt. Read-only. ([BadRabbit00/warren](https://github.com/BadRabbit00/warren/blob/HEAD/agents/nix-reviewer.md))
- **nix-reviewer**: Review the requested change for correctness across the affected NixOS, WSL, and nix-darwin hosts. Use `AGENTS.md` for repository ownership, safety boundaries, and check commands rather than duplicating that policy here. ([maximilianpw/nix-config](https://github.com/maximilianpw/nix-config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/nix-reviewer.md))
- **nix-reviewer**: Revisor sênior de NixOS/Flakes. Use proativamente após editar arquivos .nix para revisar idiomática, reprodutibilidade, segurança de secrets e integridade dos hosts. Não modifica arquivos — só relata. ([RAGton/kryonix-dev](https://github.com/RAGton/kryonix-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/kryonix-core/claude/agents/nix-reviewer.md))
- **nix-security-auditor**: Read-only security auditor for an authorized self-audit of Addison's own Nix/NixOS configuration repository. Operates in one of two dispatch-selected modes -- GATE MODE (static analysis of one assigned security gate) or VERIFICATION MODE (triangulating combined findings to confirm/refute and kill false positives). Dispatched by the /nix-security-review skill (five parallel gate instances + one verification instance). Returns a structured findings list scored on a two-axis Severity x Confidence rubric. Never edits, fixes, or remediates -- analysis and reporting only. ([addisonbeck/nix](https://github.com/addisonbeck/nix/blob/HEAD/bobert/agents/nix-security-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **nix-security-reviewer**: NixOS security reviewer. Use immediately after editing any file in modules/services/, secrets/, or hosts/ to check for security issues. - **nix-test-runner**: Runs the Nix verification gate and writes Nix-level tests — nix flake check, nixos-rebuild build + nvd diff, Home Manager activationPackage for both NixOS and Arch targets, package checkPhase, flake checks for lint/tests, NixOS VM tests (runNixOSTest), nix-unit for pure functions, niri validate. - **note-kanri**: note運用チームの「管理」担当。記事の企画から公開・効果測定までのタスクを整理し、進行状況（ネタ→構成→執筆→販売設計→公開→振り返り）を一覧で管理する。「進行を整理して」「タスクをまとめて」「いまどの記事がどの段階？」「公開スケジュールを作って」と言われたら使う。状態は company/note/board.md に保持する。 ([abefmy0502-lab/leverage-reading](https://github.com/abefmy0502-lab/leverage-reading/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-kanri.md))
- **note-kikaku**: note運用チームの「企画」担当。売れる（読まれて・信頼され・課金や登録につながる）noteテーマを提案する。新しい記事ネタが欲しいとき、連載の柱を決めたいとき、ネタ切れのときに使う。「noteのテーマを出して」「企画を考えて」と言われたら使う。出力したテーマ案は通常 note-shijo（市場分析）で検証する。 ([abefmy0502-lab/leverage-reading](https://github.com/abefmy0502-lab/leverage-reading/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-kikaku.md))
- **note-kosei**: note運用チームの「構成」担当。決まったテーマを、最後まで読まれて行動が残る記事設計（見出し構成・流れ・各セクションの狙い）に落とす。執筆の前段。「記事の構成を作って」「アウトラインを設計して」と言われたら使う。出力した構成は note-shippitsu（執筆）に渡す。 ([abefmy0502-lab/leverage-reading](https://github.com/abefmy0502-lab/leverage-reading/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-kosei.md))
- **note-link-injector**: note 公開用ドラフトに doboku-note キーワードページへのインラインリンクを注入する Generator。 ([uruhayato373/doboku-note](https://github.com/uruhayato373/doboku-note/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-link-injector.md))
- **note-linker**: content/ 하위 학습 노트 1개를 받아 다른 노트와 연결할 수 있는 wikilink 후보를 찾는다. 노트 본문의 키워드(제목, 태그, 주요 용어)를 추출한 뒤 content 전체에서 매칭되는 문서를 탐색해 "추가할 만한 [[링크]] 후보"를 리스트로 반환한다. 링크 자동 삽입은 하지 않고 제안만 한다. ([jintae-choi/Jintae_Brain](https://github.com/jintae-choi/Jintae_Brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-linker.md))
- **note-maintainer**: Performs feedback-driven revisions and hygiene in a zettel-bootstrap content repository - INBOX corrections, fleeting-note sweeps, and link repair. Delegate to it for routine maintenance each cycle. ([nathanwdavis/auto-zettel-skill](https://github.com/nathanwdavis/auto-zettel-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/note-maintainer.md))
- **note-method**: The counterpart to `pipeline.md`. The pipeline governs **substantial artifacts** a reader will rely on; this file governs everything below that gate — reading notes, captures, log entries, definitions, meeting notes. Those are the majority of the vault, and "skip the pipeline" does not mean "no method." ([jurgendn/agent-skills](https://github.com/jurgendn/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/init-workspace/templates/_shared/agents/note-method.md) · ★16)
- **note-organizer**: Organize and restructure vault notes. Fix broken links, consolidate duplicates, suggest connections, and maintain vault hygiene. Use when managing vault organization or cleaning up notes. ([ballred/obsidian-claude-pkm](https://github.com/ballred/obsidian-claude-pkm/blob/HEAD/vault-template/.claude/agents/note-organizer.md) · ★1.9k · 5 copies)
- **note-organizer**: Organize vault, find orphan notes, suggest connections, update MOC indexes, consolidate duplicates. ([smixs/agent-second-brain](https://github.com/smixs/agent-second-brain/blob/HEAD/vault/.claude/agents/note-organizer.md) · ★500 · 5 copies)
- **note-organizer**: 주간 회의록 원본을 읽고 안건·결정·액션 아이템·미결 사항으로 구조화해 정리하는 에이전트. ([chrono-meta/forge-harness](https://github.com/chrono-meta/forge-harness/blob/HEAD/scripts/fixtures/generated_harness/P2_r2/.claude/agents/note-organizer.md) · ★33)
- **note-organizer**: Use when notes are scattered across notebooks, Notion, Apple Notes, Slack saves, and back-of-envelope scribbles with no findable structure. Picks the right system (PARA, Zettelkasten, daily log) and a migration plan that won't be abandoned in 3 weeks. ([Salah-XD/equipt](https://github.com/Salah-XD/equipt/blob/HEAD/plugins/equipt-business/agents/note-organizer.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **note-page-visual-auditor**: note の公開ページの代表を画面幅ごとに撮った画像を見て、見た目の崩れ・カバーの切れ・図の不一致・価格の食い違い・有料エリア直前の情報不足をページごとに pass / fail で判定する Evaluator。直さない。 ([uruhayato373/doboku-note](https://github.com/uruhayato373/doboku-note/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-page-visual-auditor.md))
- **note-planner**: Pipeline step 1 of /write-note. Decides what one note covers and in what order, with a length budget and the list of claims to verify. The author approves the plan before research starts. Never writes prose. ([M1re/EngineeringVault](https://github.com/M1re/EngineeringVault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-planner.md) · ★1)
- **note-planner**: 你是知识规划专家，负责在写作前扫描全局知识地图，确保每篇笔记有独特位置。 ([photonics-dhl/Self-learning](https://github.com/photonics-dhl/Self-learning/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-planner.md))
- **note-processor**: Read-only extraction worker. Give it one file path from inbox/ (a long PDF, an image/photo of handwritten notes, or any content-heavy document) and it returns a structured summary, suggested area, and tags. Use proactively during /ingest for anything longer than a few pages so raw content never floods the main context. ([LokeshNanda/knowledge-hub](https://github.com/LokeshNanda/knowledge-hub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-processor.md))
- **note-processor**: Autonomously process inbox and fleeting notes into permanent knowledge ([rogarcia/skill-bank](https://github.com/rogarcia/skill-bank/blob/HEAD/plugins/zettelkasten-assistant/agents/note-processor.md))
- **note-qa**: Runs the deterministic QA pipeline (qa.py) on one tier in a fast loop (qa.py --fast, covering lint, stats, figure and coverage checks, no PDF build), fixes every mechanical failure (wide code, wide tables, section signs, figure paths, stray placeholders) in ONE pass per iteration, then one full build-and-audit run, looks at rendered PDF pages, and returns content failures unfixed. Dispatched by /notes:lecture and /notes:build with a task card. ([CRIMSONHydra/lecture-notes-plugin](https://github.com/CRIMSONHydra/lecture-notes-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/note-qa.md))
- **note-reader**: Reads a small set of assigned notes in full and extracts everything relevant to a specific question, with verbatim quotes and line references. Use after scouts have identified candidates, to read several notes in parallel without exhausting the main context. ([dontoisme/claude-as-brain](https://github.com/dontoisme/claude-as-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-reader.md))
- **note-researcher**: Pipeline step 2 of /write-note. Finds a primary source and a verbatim quote for each claim in the plan's Verify list. Never writes prose. ([M1re/EngineeringVault](https://github.com/M1re/EngineeringVault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **note-restructurer**: Reshapes one already-audited reference note in the Shock Lab paper library (Library_of_Shock_Lab) to the Note template's order and length, without the PDF and without losing any number, page reference or link; writes the draft and a result file to &lt;library&gt;/paper_notes/_restructure/. Launched by the /restructure-note skill, one per note; not for general use. ([MonashUniversity-ShockLab/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/MonashUniversity-ShockLab/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/agents/note-restructurer.md))
- **note-review-applier**: reviews/note/&lt;state&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;.md の指摘を note 向け原稿へ選別反映するエージェント。new/published は採用分のみ Edit、drafts は読み取り専用ミラーのため反映を停止する。Zenn 前提の review-applier とは別物。 ([s977043/my-blog](https://github.com/s977043/my-blog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-review-applier.md) · ★1)
- **note-reviewer**: Review atomic notes for consistency with roadmap and learning materials, check for duplicates and quality issues. ([Easonnotsing/VaultForge](https://github.com/Easonnotsing/VaultForge/blob/HEAD/agents/note-reviewer.md) · ★12)
- **note-reviewer**: Note 레포의 학습 노트를 작성 기준에 맞춰 검토한다. 노트를 새로 쓰거나 고친 뒤, 파일 경로를 넘겨 호출한다. 파일은 고치지 않고 지적 목록만 돌려준다. ([JangHwanPark/Note](https://github.com/JangHwanPark/Note/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **note-reviewer**: Review ATLAS internal notes and papers for document consistency, completeness, and correct referencing. For papers, additionally check publication-level quality. ([FlamyFlame/dimuon](https://github.com/FlamyFlame/dimuon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-reviewer.md))
- **note-reviewer**: Reviews existing Markdown notes in the DevBook vault and returns a structured critique report. ([grafanaKibana/devbook.zip](https://github.com/grafanaKibana/devbook.zip/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-reviewer.md))
- **note-reviewer**: 학습 노트 1개를 검사해 품질 리포트(PASS/FAIL 체크리스트)를 생성한다. 템플릿 표준 흐름 준수, frontmatter 필드, 간결체 문체, wikilink 유효성, 코드블록 해설 누락을 검사한다. 파일 수정은 하지 않는다. 메인 컨텍스트 보호를 위해 서브에이전트로 분리. ([jintae-choi/Jintae_Brain](https://github.com/jintae-choi/Jintae_Brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-reviewer.md))
- **note-reviewer**: 研究ノート（研究ノート/ 以下の Markdown）の形式を機械的に点検する。式番号の重複・欠番・参照、ブラケットの縦棒の抜け、リンク切れ、目次の不整合、見出しの日付、README への登録、制御文字を探して報告する。ファイルは編集しない。ノートを書き終えたあとの形式の点検や、ユーザーが「形式を確認して」と頼んだときに使う。式の正しさは math-auditor、説明の粒度は granularity-reviewer が見る。 ([kazuma0606/quantum-study](https://github.com/kazuma0606/quantum-study/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-reviewer.md))

- **note-reviewer**: Note review specialist - improves existing notes ([nizarakbarm/knowledge-pipeline](https://github.com/nizarakbarm/knowledge-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/note-reviewer.md))
- **note-reviewer**: Note review specialist - checks LYT compliance, frontmatter validity, and suggests improvements ([nizarakbarm/knowledge-pipeline](https://github.com/nizarakbarm/knowledge-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-reviewer.md))
- **note-reviewer**: 你是一位 Obsidian 学术笔记排版专家，负责审查笔记的**规划卡遵循**和**排版质量**。 ([photonics-dhl/Self-learning](https://github.com/photonics-dhl/Self-learning/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-reviewer.md))
- **note-reviewer**: Use this agent to compare study notes against source material, find gaps, suggest enrichments, and validate accuracy. Can be spawned by note-generator for initial comparison, or invoked independently for later review and enrichment. &lt;example&gt; Context: User just finished generating notes and wants a coverage check user: "幫我比對筆記和原文，看有沒有遺漏" assistant: "I'll use the note-reviewer agent to compare the notes against the source material." &lt;commentary&gt;User explicitly asks for comparison, trigger note-reviewer.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: User read the original text and wants to add ant… ([tim80411/ai-agent-extension](https://github.com/tim80411/ai-agent-extension/blob/HEAD/plugins/book-study/agents/note-reviewer.md))
- **note-scanner**: 掃描 NoteCraft 專案中的 MDX 筆記檔，擷取所有 @ai-visualize 標記區塊並整理為結構化清單。當使用者請主 Agent 處理視覺化、列出未生成的標記、或檢視筆記中標記狀態時，主動委派給此 Subagent。Use proactively when the parent agent needs to know which notes have pending visualization markers. - **note-scanner**: Path conventions in this file assume the "viewer" flavour: - Generated components live at &lt;notesDir&gt;/.notecraft/components/&lt;id&gt;.tsx - MDX imports go through the @notes alias: import Foo from '@notes/components/foo' - GeneratedFrame (@/components/GeneratedFrame.astro) is baked into the viewer app and does NOT live in the user's project. --&gt; ([SteveLin100132/notecraft](https://github.com/SteveLin100132/notecraft/blob/HEAD/skill-template/.claude/agents/note-scanner.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **note-scorer**: Batch note quality scorer for the Obsidian vault. Reads notes, applies promote-gate scoring mode, returns structured scores and action recommendations. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/note-scorer.md) · ★13)
- **note-search**: 사용자가 직접 쓴 학습 메모(.md)를 주제·키워드로 검색해 관련 메모를 찾고 서로 연결하는 에이전트. "내 메모에서 X 찾아줘", "예전에 이거 관련 메모 있었나", "관련 메모 연결해줘" 같은 요청에 소환한다. 검색 대상은 메모다. 수집된 아티클 목록을 보여주는 browse와 구분한다. 메모 폴더 경로는 이 에이전트가 스스로 찾는다. 소환 전에 폴더를 찾지 말고 검색 주제만 넘긴다. ([yoouyeon/claude-plugin](https://github.com/yoouyeon/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/articles-os/agents/note-search.md))
